- **knowledge-distillation**: def distill_loss(student_logits, teacher_logits, labels, T=2.0, alpha=0.5): kd_loss = F.kl_div( F.log_softmax(student_logits / T, dim=-1), F.softmax(teacher_logits / T, dim=-1), reduction="batchmean", ) * (T * T) ce_loss = F.cross_entropy(student_logits, labels) return alpha * kd_loss + (1 - alpha) * ce_loss ``` ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/knowledge-distillation) · ★1 · 2 copies)
